[EVENT: LBN20712]
Date: 2023-10-01
Country: Lebanon | Location: Tyr
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Lebanon) [Rioters]
Actor2: Civilians (Lebanon) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: L'Orient Le Jour [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 October 2023, masked men armed with knives and some with firearms attacked a pub and beat up some of its staff in Tyr (Tyr), over homophobic reasons. The attack came after people at the pub shared stories on social media that included the rainbow flag. The masked men shouted at customers and hit several tables.

[EVENT: LBN15131]

Actor2: Military Forces of Lebanon (2021-) [State forces]

[EVENT: LBN15138]
Date: 2023-10-05
Country: Lebanon | Location: Ed Daoura
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Lebanon) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Syria) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Naharnet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 5 October 2023, physical clashes erupted between locals and Syrian workers of a nearby sewing factory in Ed Daoura (Al Matn), after a local was insulted by the Syrians following a car accident. Following the fight around 600 locals tried to storm the factory where the Syrian workers were hiding. The army deployed and arrested 8 Syrians who were involved. They had no residency papers.
